# Every act in this repository

1364 acts, 1,374 versions. Each act lives in a folder named by its official identifier, `<kind>/<year>/<number>/` (the law in State Gazette issue 29 of 2010, National Assembly record 9940, is `zakon/2010/dv029-9940/`); its README.md lists its articles and the decisions made by hand. Dates are in-force days. Generated on every publish; the same list is in [data/acts.json](data/acts.json).
